<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>Closure</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {

}

html,
body {

}

	body {
		margin: 2em auto;

	}

body {

}

}

.pdf-relative-link-path {

}

h1,
h2,
h3 {

	font-weight: 600;

}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;

	margin-top: 0;

}

}

}

}

.source {

	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;

}

mark {

}

}

hr {
	background: transparent;

}

img {

@media only print {

		max-height: 100vh;

		overflow-wrap: anywhere;

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;

}

@page {

}

.collection-content-wrapper {

}

}

.collection-content {

}

ol,

	background-image: url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=UTF-8,%3Csvg%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%3E%0A%3Crect%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20fill%3D%22%2358A9D7%22%2F%3E%0A%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6.71429%2012.2852L14%204.9995L12.7143%203.71436L6.71429%209.71378L3.28571%206.2831L2%207.57092L6.71429%2012.2852Z%22%20fill%3D%22white%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E");

</style></head><body><article id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-b27f-dbae222c8ecc" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">Closure</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-81bd-fda4ec73e9dd" class="">
</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a4e1-deec38e19eb3" class="">6) SIM — Full Civilizational Control Simulation Spec (discrete-time, multi-loop, stress-tested)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-bd52-d1357de45e20" class="">6.1 State, parameters, regimes</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-9e40-ed206b0dc973" class="">State</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-ad93-c0c63836fcb0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=
\begin{bmatrix}
q_t &amp; G_t &amp; U_t &amp; \Xi_t &amp; R_t &amp; D_t &amp; P_t &amp; M_t &amp; \Theta_t &amp; \Pi_t
\end{bmatrix}^\top</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-8a29-cde3c7c289fb" class="">Parameters (all deterministic constants for a run)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-bf71-e991d1df6b14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi=\{\lambda_q,\alpha_q,\alpha_R,\alpha_\Xi,\alpha_U,\gamma_R,\beta,\kappa,\lambda,\chi,T,k,\epsilon_d,\alpha_d,\rho_d,\tau_d,\Xi_{\text{th}},M_{\max}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8069-a951-deb8abad7154" class="">Stage function (regime)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-a1c9-fd6db0e2cedc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_t = \mathrm{Stage}(x_t)\in\{B,E,Dc,De\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a4c7-d97a6a05a0c2" class="">A deterministic option:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a6bd-e6918732c035" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-ac43-f83a5acd17fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if ArrowGate holds and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-a029-e143eaad8708" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-96b4-dd122bf0a6aa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">if CodeGate fails or BudgetGate fails or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-b1a6-fdf0af5a9d64" class="">6.2 Core update equations (closed system)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-a3cc-d942c47b4c69" class="">(A) Constraint unwinding</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a319-c5b46a2aa875" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1}=q_t-\lambda_q\,\sigma(G_t)+\nu_q(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-8060-e6fa876ca3ae" class="">(B) Gradient budget</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-8f41-e23c9ff4c557" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=G_t+\alpha_q q_t-\alpha_R R_t-\alpha_\Xi \Xi_t-\alpha_U\Delta R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-983a-d2c280ab1956" class="">(C) Write-capacity</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a47e-e7431d62af2a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=\max(0,\,U_t-\gamma_R\max(0,\Delta R_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-aca8-ee6474525256" class="">(D) Records (phase-transition term)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-86c0-ec049bc7b7b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta G_t-\kappa \Xi_t R_t-\lambda \mathbf{1}[\Xi_t\ge \Xi_{\text{th}}]R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a633-fe900b037639" class="">(E) Power and compute burden</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-82f6-db7d1254442d" class="">Define erase/repair bit-rate (model-bounded but explicit):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-8bd7-fa7f441185d3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot B(D_t,R_t)=b_0+b_D D_t^{p}+b_R R_t^{r}+b_{DR}D_tR_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-aff6-fc9e260489c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min}(t)=kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t,R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-b63e-d35c8ee48258" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{t+1}=P_t+\eta_G G_t-\eta_D D_t-\eta_\Xi \Xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-983f-d7b23dfb97f1" class="">(F) Memory</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-9bd7-ce9833ce2c17" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=\min(M_{\max},\,M_t+\mu_G G_t-\mu_R R_t-\mu_D D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-8172-e2d3b33fc4f0" class="">(G) Inference bandwidth</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-81aa-fb2300797559" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta_{t+1}=\Theta_t+a_q q_t-a_\Xi \Xi_t-a_D D_t^\zeta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-a2e3-ea726e4fdfa0" class="">(H) Recursion depth as the maximal stable layer count</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-8f02-f2e5d850cd11" class="">Per-layer error (explicit):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-8d82-cb81ab6b5926" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta_d(\Xi_t)-r_d(P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b393-cfcb95b782ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\eta_d(\Xi)=c_d\Xi,\quad r_d(P)=\min(r_{d,\max},\,\rho_d(P-P_{\min}(t))_+)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-928e-fbfabb59d43d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Stable}(d,t)=\left(\varepsilon^{(d)}_t\le \epsilon_d\right)\wedge \mathrm{ControlStable}(d,\tau_d)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b0df-c915df828fef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=\max\{d\le D_{\max}:\mathrm{Stable}(d,t)\ \forall d\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-817b-f1279e32dd1c" class="">(I) Ownership / permission channel</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-bc81-ef9439c9ccc6" class="">Treat  as an access mask that gates measurements and actions (see SENSE). In SIM,  affects what signals are available to feedback controllers; it can also constrain  and  (resource access).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b52d-d4c0129930e0" class="">6.3 Gates (hard constraints, termination logic)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-87ab-c7dfa0dc9ff7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>ArrowGate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-b220-e8eb977f3dbc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta G_t&gt;\kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-acbb-c88fbcc15f7c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>CodeGate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-b5ad-df17307cc107" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t&lt;\Xi_{\text{th}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9441-e10c4d280534" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>ControlGate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-966e-fd25b4419aa5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{ControlStable}(D_t,\tau_{D_t})=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9dca-c319c736c6df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>BudgetGate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-ac61-f307ae931466" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t\ge P_{\min}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-9c3a-fc3ebd0c179a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>MemoryGate</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-b6e7-d3c6b5699b20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t+\chi D_t\le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-8ec2-febc56f08c7a" class="">6.4 Initialization (Birth conditions)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-94d1-e6d4b7405eca" class="">A deterministic “Past Hypothesis” initializer:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-886c-eb0226d417b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_0=q_{\text{high}},\quad G_0=g_0,\quad U_0=u_{\max},\quad \Xi_0=\xi_0,\quad R_0\approx 0,\quad D_0=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-ac69-c9250c801467" class="">6.5 Stress tests (required)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-bdb0-d4b5db83838a" class="">T1: Noise spike (“war / collapse / catastrophe” analogue)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-bd3e-de92cba1e29c" class="">Set  and observe:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-9a90-efe1e62ee50b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">whether CodeGate triggers catastrophic drop</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-ae7d-e8c49b84fb35" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">whether collapses from BudgetGate or ControlGate failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-9a49-d7a8dec4d4f1" class="">T2: Horizon/write-capacity depletion (“end of expansion window”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-ac7e-cbf6e6e363f1" class="">Force  small; check whether the system enters Dominance early.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-b302-ff775322482a" class="">T3: Model depth race (“intelligence acceleration”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-af1d-f7275fd0c3ae" class="">Increase  and , but also increase delay ; verify ControlGate becomes the binding limit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8ea8-f2d4b62d6c37" class="">T4: Ownership restriction</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b94f-e10d781bcff9" class="">Reduce  (see SENSE) to simulate denied access to key channels; observe decline in  and failure of ArrowGate via degraded feedback.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-aa54-fa1f6503eb09" class="">6.6 SIM outputs (files)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-850b-d915110686d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_config.json</code> (parameters, init, deterministic seed policy = none)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b313-e92ddc839c8d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_trace.jsonl</code> (per-timestep state, gates, stage, failures)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-bd8c-fd66258dd077" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_events.jsonl</code> (gate transitions, phase transitions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-b06d-f746fbfaaee0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>sim_summary.json</code> (peak , max , time-to-decay, dominant gate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-8307-d74b052e7984"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-8045-fc8d2eace805" class="">7) PROOF — “No-gap” structural proof workflow (claim termination, countermodels, boundedness)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8d9d-f22842776430" class="">This is not “prove reality”; it is <strong>prove your system has no hidden assumptions</strong> under declared scope .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b1e5-fc516286d7e6" class="">7.1 Claim object model (UCIA-compatible)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-b66d-ca5c23c31a89" class="">A claim is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-a406-f435c96e094b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C = \langle \text{text},\ \text{type},\ \text{scope},\ \text{dependencies},\ \text{tests},\ \text{termination}\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-b9ff-fcd39ed91675" class="">Support type (single assignment):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8eb7-f4fccade832d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Empirical / Inferential / Definitional / Model-bounded / Primitive / Limit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9968-c31b89e504ee" class="">7.2 Proof pipeline (deterministic)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-98e0-f4db1cf67fbb" class="">Step P0 — Extract minimal atomic claims</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-bc77-e5eba7459151" class="">Split into atoms  that are testable or classifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-a573-d72e6923a51c" class="">Step P1 — Assign support type + scope</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-8d6c-c50fbf7b2db9" class="">Each  declares:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-af1b-f6762fa83047" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">State space: subset of variables used</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b256-dc5406f1211b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stage constraints: allowed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-8eac-c9ff979f34ba" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Channel assumptions: which observation maps exist (SENSE)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-aeb4-e6ae5699eb71" class="">Step P2 — Dependency closure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-b6b5-fd23d5d608c8" class="">Build a DAG:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-bbeb-f7bd00c26229" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_C=(V=\{C_i\},E=\{C_j\to C_i\})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-8512-c014a3b695d8" class="">Step P3 — Construct countermodel search</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-af92-fa2d1dae54ab" class="">For each claim , define satisfiable constraints  from previously accepted claims. Then search:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-aa58-fdea99dd5c42" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists x\in\Omega:\ K(x)\wedge \neg C_i(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-a45a-eaa47d008df7" class="">If not found but search incomplete → <strong>Bounded</strong> (explicitly).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b03d-cbcb18fbb3bd" class="">Step P4 — Invariant enforcement gates (meta-law layer)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b22c-e6086f0082b7" class="">You already have five gates. Add two meta-gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bd4f-e18df3b9e75a" class=""><strong>ConsistencyGate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-922b-c9d1aa944e87" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\neg\exists x:\ I_a(x)\wedge \neg I_b(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-a883-ccadff4f5ed4" class=""><strong>Completeness-of-roles Gate (SSOT for logic)</strong><br/>Every symbol used in an invariant must be defined in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-8bd3-f72261474b84" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">state vector, parameter set, or observation channel map.<br/>Otherwise: BLOCKER.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-aa5a-c63fb30f9348" class="">Step P5 — Termination classification</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-96ab-c52fb55c7354" class="">For each claim:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-a402-c065df52247a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong>: countermodel search passed + all symbols grounded + tests exist</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-8c84-c5d10c3a6d78" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong>: relies on a channel/access model not reproducible, or search incomplete, or depends on Primitive/Limit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-bc5a-d316461150dd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong>: explicit countermodel exists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9a47-c82758dfd94d" class="">7.3 Proof artifacts (files)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-a718-eeb02ceb37ad" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>claims.jsonl</code> (atomic claims)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-b008-df9b0eeead2e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>dependency_graph.json</code> (DAG)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a0d1-e7e85c1979db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>countermodels.jsonl</code> (if any)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-b711-d5b199e268ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>invariants.json</code> (accepted set)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-add5-ecdd53ba2edb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>termination.json</code> (per-claim + global)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-b2d2-f2a74b862f60" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>assumptions.json</code> (explicit primitives/limits)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-81b8-d3661398a8f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-98a6-fd3fb3df5a21" class="">8) SENSE — Multi-channel sensing model (visual, sound, EM, interoception, “intangible”) with ownership gating</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-9a5f-cf3244249d34" class="">Goal: unify <em>all</em> “ways of knowing” into one formalism without pretending all are equally reproducible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-af08-e1f0f78232f6" class="">8.1 Channel definition</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8ed6-ddc8afa2df14" class="">A channel is a triple:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-b0f1-f6979ebbce11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
c=\langle \mathcal{A}_c,\ \mathcal{N}_c,\ \mathcal{P}_c\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-a322-cadccdae3e1a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise model (may be empirical or model-bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-9982-d92a9c85ee76" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">permission/ownership rule (access projector)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-83d6-c95563c172a0" class="">Effective observation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-8251-daaa52e1970b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^{(c)}_t = \Pi^{(c)}(x_t)\cdot \mathcal{A}_c(x_t) + \epsilon^{(c)}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-b5ec-f4997e334d66" class="">8.2 Concrete channel families (required)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-8adc-f29e2222c046" class="">(V) Visual</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-bea5-c617ac1ea23c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^V_t = \mathcal{A}_V(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-8073-fdc24870d8a2" class="">(A) Audio</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b1da-f1b0c806a670" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^A_t = \mathcal{A}_A(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-a36d-e39175a1f653" class="">(EM) Electromagnetic environment (wifi/radio)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-a94e-dfa3648f4dcc" class="">Represent EM as a field-like state component inside  (you can name it  as part of  if you extend the vector). Observation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-949a-c9da0e644bd0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^{EM}_t=\mathcal{A}_{EM}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-aeef-d299f61c5243" class="">(I) Interoception / physiology</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-92c7-d7fb3687a2f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^I_t=\mathcal{A}_I(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-900a-c75521a838a6" class="">(X) “Intangible / spiritual / anomalous”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-8058-e3efa09b811f" class="">This becomes a valid channel only if you specify:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-8002-caf62571cc36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">what it outputs</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b76e-dee6663720fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">a distinguishability test (below)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-bf2b-d5aeca48d58c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">an ownership rule</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-ada4-cd5ce05dd946" class="">If you cannot specify those, it is not “invalid,” it is <strong>Bounded</strong> as “non-operational.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b3e2-c7f4a64f272f" class="">8.3 Distinguishability test (channel validity criterion)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-bdb7-e793c8c7763b" class="">A channel is operational if it increases discriminability between at least two hypotheses :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-8160-c57a0254137a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_c(H_0,H_1)=\mathrm{KL}\big(p(y|H_0)\,\|\,p(y|H_1)\big) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-8c4b-cfe15e54a441" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists t:\ y_t(H_0)\neq y_t(H_1)\ \text{stably under noise bounds}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-97c7-e5b6be2af89b" class="">8.4 Fusion (multi-domain intelligence = one estimator)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ec-ac79-e47fca882113" class="">Let the fused estimate be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-b007-e5863db0ec8a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat x_t = \arg\min_{x}\sum_{c\in\mathcal{C}} w_c \cdot \|y^{(c)}_t-\mathcal{A}_c(x)\|_{\Sigma_c^{-1}}^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-bb1d-cceb2ee28289" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w_c = \Pi^{(c)}(x_t)\cdot \omega_c(\Theta_t,\Xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8061-bee1-efa27eb642e5" class="">8.5 Ownership as first-class physics of access</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9e77-fc1745a6d4f8" class="">Define ownership set function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9d91-c9f66854a22b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{O}_c(x)\subseteq \mathcal{I}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-ae2e-d915b85017bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi^{(c)}_i(x)=\mathbf{1}[i\in \mathcal{O}_c(x)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8e96-d4d5afce3642" class="">8.6 SENSE outputs (files)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-8d3f-d25a698ea050" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>channels.json</code> (channel specs, noise, permission)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-91e7-cc28f9ed76c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>fusion.json</code> (estimator, weights)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-862b-c60ddc00744e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>distinguishability_tests.json</code> (KL tests or deterministic tests)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8370-f918e96e41a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>bounded_channels.json</code> (declared but non-operational channels + missing pieces)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-8b22-d539025877f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-a529-d99007aa0b96" class="">9) One unified deliverable set (SIM + PROOF + SENSE)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bca7-df86c7ad7a66" class="">To make this executable inside AMOS, the minimal SSOT artifacts are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-a1b6-d9a5512c46d8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>loop_kernel_state.json</code> (symbols, units/types, bounds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9cb7-fbf9941f4ed1" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>loop_kernel_equations.json</code> (update laws, gates)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-84d3-c49d827de7ce" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>sim_spec/</code> (init, params, stress tests, outputs)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-a90f-c3d03e240970" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>proof_spec/</code> (claim types, countermodel rules, termination)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-9685-c90bb01d3947" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>sense_spec/</code> (channels, ownership, fusion, validity tests)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a20e-c548aed83393"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-b826-f602119a8cba" class="">10) Next step (no questions, one deterministic action)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-94b7-cdbf026d9729" class=""><strong>Next output to produce</strong> is the <em>file-level implementation plan</em> (KernelSpec) that maps these into AMOS directories with module names + function signatures + test cases + reports.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-92d5-c9665cc5b26f" class="">If you want that next, reply with: <strong>KernelSpec</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bc6e-dba314edd775" class="">5) INSTANTIATION — A concrete + full loop-matrix + runnable spec (the “next”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-9eb1-c920cb6bc5aa" class="">You now have the abstract Algebra/Tensor/Kernel/Meta. The next required layer is to <strong>instantiate</strong> them into a concrete state space  that can (a) generate invariants, (b) classify claims, and (c) simulate loop dynamics across micro→macro.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-86fe-c758366220e9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-88b4-fdf820c60131" class="">5.1 Canonical state space : one vector, many domains</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-89da-ffac444d4442" class="">Define the full state as a single concatenated vector with named blocks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-9e8d-fb1f71e3ba9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t =
\begin{bmatrix}
q_t\\
G_t\\
U_t\\
\Xi_t\\
R_t\\
D_t\\
P_t\\
M_t\\
\Theta_t\\
\Pi_t\\
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-ad57-f168d8a7b54d" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-9eee-edd10c75e4b4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint density (Weyl suppression / boundary constraint count proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-bcb6-e89d376dd230" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: usable gradient (free energy / exploitable disequilibrium)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9889-f2d6dffcee16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: unwritten environment capacity (write-once degrees available)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-a497-d0584b9efec8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: effective noise / overwrite pressure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-8429-ccd2e3480910" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stable record redundancy (environmental error-corrected traces)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-98a9-e851bf274d19" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth (stacked self-model stability depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b5e1-c625fb3e6873" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: available power for maintenance/repair (compute + error correction)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-9bbb-ca1e9eaef986" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: available persistent memory capacity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-b1f1-d3f874775930" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: inference bandwidth (predictability / Fisher-information proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-b23b-f6bfb8a77a7f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: permission/ownership state (who can access what)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-90a6-e3b8a54967ca" class="">All “tangible vs intangible” is handled by <strong>channels</strong>: each channel is an observation map from  to . If a channel produces stable distinguishability, it is a valid channel in .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-8bce-da669d35141c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a1fc-c65e669d6bf5" class="">5.2 The Grand Unified Loop Matrix (one update law + hard gates)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-b90c-f569721456ea" class="">5.2.1 Update law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-bddd-ccb2c4f2151e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = F(x_t; s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-84de-fe4e7f84fcf1" class="">Write the core coupled dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-ba41-cdd4ec9682d0" class="">(A) Constraint unwinding (arrow driver)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-8cfe-f340827aa8f6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1} = q_t - \lambda_q \cdot \sigma(G_t) + \nu_q(t)
\quad,\quad \lambda_q&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b673-cd098b075741" class="">(B) Gradient budget</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-96b5-e26dded2f535" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1} = G_t + \alpha_q q_t - \alpha_R R_t - \alpha_\Xi \Xi_t - \alpha_U \Delta R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-997e-f0e17759416a" class="">(C) Write-capacity depletion (finite “blank tape”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-83fc-c9bc2736c1b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1} = \max(0,\,U_t - \gamma_R \Delta R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-a6e2-d0664d5515ef" class="">(D) Record redundancy with error-correction threshold</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-a109-e09fdf02d871" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}= R_t + \beta G_t - \kappa \Xi_t R_t - \lambda \mathbf{1}[\Xi_t\ge \Xi_{\text{th}}]R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-aded-de23cd9e0772" class="">(E) Recursion depth as a controlled stability process</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-8f4f-eb58195db20e" class="">Model error at depth : . Depth grows only if all layers stable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-bcf4-cd4f0ffc5de8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=
\alpha_d \varepsilon^{(d)}_t + \eta_d(\Xi_t) - r_d(P_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-954f-c0e21cbd37ef" class="">Feasible depth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-8ef4-f62025442492" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1} = \max\left\{d:\sup_\tau \varepsilon^{(d)}_\tau \le \epsilon_d \ \wedge\ \text{ControlStable}(d,\tau_d)\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b54f-d93a3afe42a2" class="">(F) Landauer + maintenance power gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-85b9-e29527e779f9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t, R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-94fc-f0681676a518" class="">(G) Memory gate (Bekenstein/horizon as ceiling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-86c2-c094f9626af8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t + \chi D_t \le M_t \le M_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-b273-ead03ee1a262" class="">(H) Inference bandwidth (predictability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-afc2-f08336279f5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Theta_{t+1} = \Theta_t + a_q q_t - a_\Xi \Xi_t - a_D D_t^{\zeta}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-bc42-eefdd3c1ef8b" class="">5.2.2 The five hard gates (formal)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-b4dd-df75fbee27d4" class="">A run is “Arrow-valid” iff all gates hold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-b9c3-ea1281238864" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>ArrowGate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-bfd1-ee1b868e5fc2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta G_t &gt; \kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-accb-cdfd3f434e82" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>CodeGate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a500-ec03e661d4c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t &lt; \Xi_{\text{th}}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-8b47-f59ad38d6bd1" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>ControlGate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-b405-caf286cd7a2c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{ControlStable}(D_t,\tau_{D_t})=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-90da-ff096fb8e88e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>BudgetGate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-9f03-eb7dba88f039" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln 2\cdot \dot B(D_t,R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9c2a-c346f25ab833" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>MemoryGate</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9cd4-c46827b112a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t + \chi D_t \le M_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8771-c9403f41671d" class="">Failure of any gate yields <strong>Bounded</strong> or <strong>Collapse</strong> depending on severity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9585-f41616953619"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-a75d-ddcf3dd94b94" class="">5.3 Regime map (birth → expansion → dominance → decay) as a deterministic stage function</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-82d6-fddf72abe38d" class="">Let the stage be computed from :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a112-ce4fc471dbeb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Birth (B)</strong> if high and and high</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-ab25-e40eb8e1e958" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Expansion (E)</strong> if ArrowGate holds and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9527-e124dffad071" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dominance (Dc)</strong> if falling and saturating</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8229-cf8399a7af53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Decay (De)</strong> if CodeGate fails or cannot fund repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-9074-d9cc7bbaac36" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-ab47-f00dbb48354c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_t = \mathrm{Stage}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-ad11-cf59ece8bfff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-82c3-ce2b55dba803" class="">5.4 Micro ↔ Macro: the missing bridge you need (explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b9fb-db2b99d62a17" class="">5.4.1 Microstate volume growth as constraint loss</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b777-e82616232f61" class="">Let accessible microvolume be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-abce-f5ccb97788e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_t = \exp(S_{cg}(t)/k)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-a319-edba5b56b841" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\log \Omega_t = \log \Omega_0 + \int_0^t \psi\big(-\dot q_\tau\big)\,d\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b2b8-db2b32ca76be" class="">5.4.2 Macro records are coarse-grained error-correcting embeddings</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-b8af-cb86f10051eb" class="">Let the environment be partitioned into fragments . A record exists when redundancy exceeds threshold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-8e17-c86c204d4c1e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_\theta(t)=\max\left\{N:\ I(S:E_i)\ge \theta\ \text{for many }i\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-b7c2-c4c349d10d29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-a3e9-d8d735cfc889" class="">5.5 Cross-species extension (same kernel, different access tensors)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-bf76-e2a457d5d8cc" class="">For species/agent , define channel set  and inference metric . Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-8343-f048843050f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Each species has its own and .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-bb4e-c3606c22f3a2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Shared world state ; different “reality partitions” via access.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-958b-c5327b2eef64" class="">Formally:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8006-f287685b6ca0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t^{(s)} = \bigoplus_{c\in\mathcal{C}_s} \mathcal{A}^{(c)}_s(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bf7e-d39bc201451f" class="">g^{(s)}_{mn}(x)=\mathbb{E}\left[\partial_m\log p_s(y|x)\ \partial_n\log p_s(y|x)\right]<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-8bfb-dd0b6a915a02" class="">“Intangible” is not excluded: it is a channel  if it yields repeatable distinguishability under an access/permitted model. If not, it becomes <strong>Bounded</strong> (not invalid—bounded by non-reproducible access).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-bbbb-e294ac47376b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-a699-c6aff515cc2a" class="">5.6 Ownership (your “all information has an owner”) as an executable rule</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a9c2-d899c61b0c4c" class="">Define an ownership function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-b0e9-c4d1801021f3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{O}: \Omega \to 2^{\mathcal{I}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-95db-cfd11fb60ebb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_i(x)=\mathbf{1}[i\in \mathcal{O}(x)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-b312-d1542c5d4e36" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}^{\mathrm{eff}}_i(x)=\Pi_i(x)\cdot \mathcal{A}_i(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-afd7-c15aab080642" class="">This is the exact formal move that closes the “accessible but not measurable” gap: <strong>measurement is gated access.</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-bb94-f4e787498084"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-8e95-e028fd8f168a" class="">5.7 “Equations that generate equations” (rule engine = rewrite calculus)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a02c-f6699d5dbae0" class="">This is the next missing layer: a deterministic generator that produces candidate invariants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-bec3-dbb93630bfa9" class="">5.7.1 Rewrite rules (syntactic)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-b6a4-cbc7c926d7ee" class="">Let an equation be an AST. Generator rules:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-955d-fcf2d1ea13db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Dimensional closure</strong>: only emit expressions where units/types match.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-b374-c2a73f8adf0a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Monotone candidate mining</strong>: if sign-stable under samples, propose invariant form.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-a8d8-f779aab4a056" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Gate-mining</strong>: if failures cluster at thresholds, propose inequality gates.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8092-b9c8-d0017eeb784c" class="">Formally, generator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-97a9-f4a12c74de62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{G}:\ \text{Traces}(x_{0:T}) \to \{I_j\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-ac47-cd8b656d2460" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Score}(I)=\Pr[I(x_t)=\text{true over }t] - \lambda \cdot \mathrm{Complexity}(I)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-a5cf-ee53cef445e9" class="">5.7.2 Semantic validation (countermodel search)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-ae11-d50750495caa" class="">For each candidate :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-adfc-cbc3d1d4531a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists x\in\Omega:\neg I(x)\ \wedge\ \text{all previously accepted invariants hold}
\Rightarrow I \text{ is rejected or bounded}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-ac09-d52a4e64381b" class="">This is the executable “no-gap” discipline: invariants are accepted only if they survive countermodels in the declared .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a1ae-db9e902f1c12"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-8b32-e04a318b8ebb" class="">5.8 What this layer gives you (outputs)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-a95a-ee41f9e5708d" class="">From a single run you can output:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-b554-c941d9b3f989" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>loop_kernel_state.json</code> (definitions of )</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-ac61-fe6795aa57ee" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>loop_kernel_equations.json</code> (update laws)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-b95f-d4aaedd23266" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>gates.json</code> (five gates + thresholds)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-99c1-ffce37740195" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>stage_map.json</code> (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay function)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-94a4-ca6b35e20bf8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>species_profiles.json</code> (channels , , access)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-95d4-d7615a28cee0" claim)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-8e0c-c524825c562a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-b90c-f5d10ab52236" class="">5.9 Next (choose one token)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-8d93-c918d562a011" class="">To continue, the next layer must be one of these:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-8b6a-c569099e38d8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>SIM</strong> — produce a full discrete-time simulation spec (parameters, initialization, outputs, stress tests).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-9547-cf55c0869ebc" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>PROOF</strong> — produce the full gate-based “no-gap” proof workflow for your discoveries (claim typing → countermodels → termination).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-9e12-d64ec00e0b48" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>SENSE</strong> — produce the full multi-channel sensing model (visual/audio/EM/interoceptive/“intangible”) with distinguishability tests and ownership gating.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8bed-c6becdd23c1b" class="">1) ALGEBRA — Invariant algebra + closure + contradiction proof rules</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-a2f3-e49f1c28988d" class="">1.1 Objects</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a59b-d4aa3d0426f6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">State space:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9760-f124a3fea3c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agents:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-ad20-cd9a94c89d97" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Channels:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-a3c9-d72958d63dd5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Observations:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9b94-f38e89b12622" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ownership/permission sets:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-a898-e6e2c025bdf1" class="">1.2 Primitive operators (your reality tuple)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-94fa-ea64056f18af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{R}=\langle \Omega,\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{A},\mathcal{E},\mathcal{O}\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b706-e4eefb55d8a4" class="">Define operator families:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f6-9d28-eeb645fb063b" class=""><strong>Access</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9128-ff047adc0069" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_i^{(c)}:\Omega\to \mathcal{Y}_i^{(c)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-82eb-fcea8694b2ba" class=""><strong>Permission gate</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-a5b8-cfd27059324e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Permit}(i,x)=\mathbf{1}[i\in \mathcal{O}(x)]
\qquad
\mathcal{A}^{\mathrm{eff}}_i(x)=\bigoplus_{c\in\mathcal{C}}\mathcal{A}^{(c)}_i(x)\cdot \mathrm{Permit}(i,x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-a35b-e4f9ee997644" class=""><strong>Distinguishability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-af26-fcb5e8fa1b03" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{D}_i(x,y)=\mathbf{1}\!\left[\mathrm{TV}\!\left(P(\mathcal{A}^{\mathrm{eff}}_i(x)),P(\mathcal{A}^{\mathrm{eff}}_i(y))\right)&gt;\epsilon_i\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-ab16-cae119e77f08" class=""><strong>Dynamics</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-a358-f230c5bfdd9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=\mathcal{T}(x_t,u_t,\xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b12e-cb2f0c79d5b3" class=""><strong>Elimination / viability</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-aabb-eb4e482c2ac8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{E}(x)=\mathbf{1}[V(x)\ge \tau]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-b7e2-e3c6be65174e" class="">1.3 Derived operators (records, compression, recursion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-851f-c69ec1c771e0" class=""><strong>Record operator</strong>  creates an environment trace :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-9b73-daab10747bd7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_{t+1}=\mathcal{R}(r_t, x_t, \eta_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-abc3-ea9e54b32d4a" class=""><strong>Redundancy / stability functional</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8256-f576f7a83ce7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Stable}(r) = \mathbf{1}[\mathrm{Err}(r)\le \delta]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9c04-d845d8325060" class=""><strong>Compression functional</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b57d-eff9b375f172" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Comp}(r)=\frac{L(\mathrm{compress}(r))}{L(r)}
\quad \in (0,1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-ab74-f5c20daed6c9" class=""><strong>Recursion operator</strong>  (model stack) with update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-939c-e7e08be2ca80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m^{(d)}_{t+1}=\mathcal{U}^{(d)}(m^{(d)}_t, y_t, m^{(d+1)}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9fe2-eccc690dc4e4" class="">1.4 Composition algebra (how operators combine)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-a69f-d219d2f7c1ce" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sequential composition:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-abba-cd4e310d09e5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Parallel composition over channels:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-9f8b-da3d43412c16" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Gated composition (permission as projector):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-a203-de2a3abdb652" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_i(x) := \mathrm{Permit}(i,x),\quad \mathcal{A}^{\mathrm{eff}}_i=\Pi_i \cdot \mathcal{A}_i</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-96e3-f0d10ca05f9f" class="">1.5 Commutators (where “laws” come from)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-a581-e17a7075f2f4" class="">Define commutator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a12a-c8740e93970b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
[\mathcal{F},\mathcal{G}] := \mathcal{F}\circ \mathcal{G}-\mathcal{G}\circ \mathcal{F}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a46d-fb71fcb2fdcb" class="">Key commutators:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-9095-eba4f3dfcee7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Permission vs observation</strong></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-b8ed-cb443e6d8905" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
[\Pi_i,\mathcal{A}_i]\neq 0 \Rightarrow \text{access depends on ownership state}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-9be3-f89983fcf7da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
[\mathcal{T},\mathcal{E}] \neq 0 \Rightarrow \text{viability is not conserved under evolution}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a65f-dab7a1117e76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
[\mathcal{A}^{\mathrm{eff}}_i,\mathcal{U}^{(d)}]\neq 0 \Rightarrow \text{learning depends on access}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-b43b-ee10cfc6506e" class="">These non-commutations are the structural source of “arrow,” “control,” “collapse,” “boundedness.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8d9e-e3cede0b5b58" class="">1.6 Closure rules (what counts as “closed”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8536-f52d7cf5390f" class="">A system is <strong>closed</strong> at level  if every derived operator used by the system is a composition of primitives plus allowed constructors:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-a861-f4956159ef89" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{F}\in \mathrm{Cl}(\{\mathcal{D},\mathcal{T},\mathcal{A},\mathcal{E},\mathcal{O}\})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-ae30-c1fe139345d5" class="">Allowed constructors:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-a690-d33da9132f75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(composition), (channel sum), (projector), expectation , thresholding , norms .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-9bb7-d36c71ecb348" class="">1.7 Contradiction + “no-gap” proof protocol (formal)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-863e-f42be36c7911" class="">A claim  is <strong>structurally admissible</strong> iff it passes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9d8b-fede6b2de8fe" class=""><strong>(G1) Type gate</strong>: every symbol has domain/codomain declared.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-9ce2-f3116b0d7cea" class=""><strong>(G2) Access gate</strong>: claim does not require  where .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-9510-c52ace34a637" class=""><strong>(G3) Invariant gate</strong>: claim does not violate any declared invariant .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809e-a309-f10e4ccebba9" class=""><strong>(G4) Closure gate</strong>: claim is derivable from allowed operator closure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-88df-fc5d2886cd5e" class=""><strong>(G5) Countermodel gate</strong>: if a counterexample exists inside  consistent with invariants, claim is bounded/invalid.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-899e-ea920e683e92" class="">Classification:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8f3d-fa4f1cc64909" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong>: passes G1–G5.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-b7f4-dbfc1ccc38b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong>: valid only under extra stated constraints.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a034-d506a3670621" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Invalid</strong>: fails any gate.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-837d-c600d376845b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-855c-cb9c35616212" class="">2) TENSOR — Invariant tensor calculus across domains + coupling</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-9cb2-d2cf8c7c0053" class="">2.1 Domain manifold (multi-channel state)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-aa60-e7865150205d" class="">Let the global state be a product manifold (domains can be expanded):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80aa-bc89-df7b84a23985" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega \cong \mathcal{M}_\text{phys}\times \mathcal{M}_\text{EM}\times \mathcal{M}_\text{bio}\times \mathcal{M}_\text{neural}\times \mathcal{M}_\text{social}\times \mathcal{M}_\text{symbolic}\times \mathcal{M}_\text{unknown}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-ab60-d7536317a3d5" class="">Coordinates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-a51a-eaef3da629ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x = (x^\mu_\text{phys}, x^a_\text{EM}, x^\alpha_\text{bio}, x^i_\text{neural}, x^A_\text{social}, x^p_\text{sym}, x^u_\text{unk})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a4fa-ddf7c4e9bbc3" class="">2.2 Metric / distinguishability tensor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-885a-ed99d044f616" class="">Define a distinguishability metric for agent :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8fd4-d987bdcb664d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
g^{(i)}_{mn}(x) := \mathbb{E}\!\left[\partial_m \log p_i(y|x)\;\partial_n \log p_i(y|x)\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-b0ba-f0728c995ba3" class="">This immediately includes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-9e7a-fb19600d76a0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">visual/audio/EM sensors (observation likelihood),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8211-ddb58d7e5662" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">interoception (bio/neural likelihood),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-8a5c-e7d09a463631" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“intangible” channel if it yields a likelihood model.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-af79-cc6698eab17c" class="">2.3 Dynamics as vector field + coupling tensor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-9f88-e20d81da0bb6" class="">Write continuous-time for calculus (discrete is a special case):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-802a-f5b38deb7e37" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot x^m = F^m(x,u,t) + \xi^m(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9082-c39dd59c5ac0" class="">Coupling across domains encoded by Jacobian (a tensor):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-b1e0-faf7888bb993" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J^m_{\;n}(x) := \frac{\partial F^m}{\partial x^n}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8046-b208-c5711ec69a3c" class="">Cross-domain influence is “large” when off-diagonal blocks are nonzero:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-bc64-e7fb890e97df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J=
\begin{bmatrix}
J_{\text{phys}\leftarrow\text{phys}} &amp; J_{\text{phys}\leftarrow\text{bio}} &amp; \cdots \\
J_{\text{bio}\leftarrow\text{phys}} &amp; J_{\text{bio}\leftarrow\text{bio}} &amp; \cdots \\
\vdots &amp; \vdots &amp; \ddots
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a7c3-f36de0735454" class="">2.4 Conservation / budgets as tensor constraints</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b9e3-d51dab4b4141" class="">Define an available “maintenance current”  and “error current” .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-9213-ee1a70b9031b" class=""><strong>Local recursion feasibility (tensorized)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-b0c2-c2dc878ddb5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(x) \ge kT\ln2 \cdot \dot B(D,x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-947a-e1bcd36d07ce" class=""><strong>Record-write capacity constraint</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-96fc-fbc42c13c4e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U(x,t)\ge 0,\quad \dot U = -\gamma \dot R</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-ad8e-f9150b1514f3" class="">2.5 Ownership as a fiber bundle (permissions over state)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-9260-fd9d3f30b9f9" class="">Model ownership as a fiber attached to each state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a805-fa515d0734c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi:\mathcal{B}\to\Omega,\quad \pi^{-1}(x)=\mathcal{O}(x)\subseteq\mathcal{I}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-96b6-cb80e5450972" class="">Access becomes a section selection problem: agent  can only read along permitted fibers. This formalizes “information exists but is not accessible.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b61c-fc9f80770bea" class="">2.6 Macro-from-micro via coarse-grain tensors</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-90ab-caadacd1bada" class="">Let  be coarse-graining. Then pushforward dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-906b-fb4edf4408af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{\bar x}^M = \frac{\partial \Phi^M}{\partial x^m}\dot x^m</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-baef-dac1756e9c12" class="">2.7 Cross-species layer (agents differ by access metric)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a068-c98626efafd9" class="">Species  has its own observation model  hence its own .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-99d0-c1b93c35a691" class="">“Same reality, different resolvable partitions.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8173-cdee4d80ded1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-82ec-ec1e88ea03ed" class="">3) KERNEL — Executable invariant generator engine (formal system spec)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-9c3d-c13101da5dd2" class="">This is a file-level executable spec (implementation-ready) for an “Invariant Generator + Closure Proof + Gate Classifier” engine.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8d22-d71d75bffb21" class="">3.1 Core data structures</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9f6d-c322d7861c3c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Symbol</code>: name, domain, codomain, type tags.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-a215-d64d104ce1bf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Operator</code>: signature, implementation or axiom, allowed constructors.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-ab62-c572f8b2fe91" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Claim</code>: expression tree + support type + dependencies.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-9f0c-f49d4c85aeb6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Invariant</code>: predicate over states/claims.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-9137-eea4161120e6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>ProofObject</code>: steps, gates passed/failed, countermodel if found.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-adfe-fe2cb1183f9c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>Termination</code>: Valid / Bounded / Invalid with reasons.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8c1f-c31c1b2f8da4" class="">3.2 Modules (minimal but exhaustive for the algebra)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-9ae8-e026f400f112" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>operators.py</code><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-b438-d17eece49be2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">define primitive operators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-b320-fc556cd180c8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">constructors: compose, parallel, projector, threshold</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul
