\mathcal{I}_a(t) = \{u:\mathrm{Access}(a,u,t)=1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a884-e65800d2f7d2" class="">But access is factorized:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-a168-f7100e87b0db" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Access}(a,u,t)= \mathrm{Policy}(\mathrm{Own}(u),a,t)\cdot \mathrm{Reach}(\mathcal{K},a,u,t)\cdot \mathrm{Decode}(a,u,t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-969f-f6e822d0712e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Policy: permission / constraint</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-b643-c899ef2f308b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reach: channel permits signal</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-8a47-eef167fb63a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decode: agent has model/keys/skills</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-89ee-f22a4678a119" class="">This is the formal closure for “all information has an owner” + “accessible via machine / nonstandard”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-9957-e1cb853cab4e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-9be8-c2b1557a0d91" class="">2) Expand the invariant families (what we didn’t list)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-b545-e184fd34d547" class="">2.1 Symmetry invariants (the missing generator class)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-a9a5-c7c88e81b24f" class="">If a transformation group  leaves dynamics invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-8d5d-f880ec82dd81" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}(g\cdot x)=g\cdot \mathcal{T}(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-853e-d7f7ab6a55a1" class="">Then any function constant on group orbits is an invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-9d53-e7b5bea0e11e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(x)=I(g\cdot x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-b7ee-e75437a97d0f" class="">This is the <strong>invariant generator</strong> that produces invariants from symmetries (physics, language, institutions).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-8ce9-cc67d149e9ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-89ec-fb4671ad77f9" class="">2.2 Causal-graph invariants (time arrow beyond entropy)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-b326-efa356dd3dc0" class="">Let  be a causal DAG over events. Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-980c-caaf8316804d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">causal depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-acc9-ee2499525435" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">irreversibility index (edge orientation stability)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-b5eb-db1832ee34b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_t := \frac{\#\text{edges whose orientation is stable under interventions}}{\#\text{edges}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-8bcd-c39bb63cff27" class="">Arrow condition becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-b6b9-d4d9475e3beb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \mathcal{A}_t &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-b9fc-df104de42c78" class="">This is “arrow = causal asymmetry that supports interventions”, not entropy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-b055-cd13ffe43c50"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-abbc-ea0c7472b4ff" class="">2.3 Compression invariants (algorithmic arrow)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-aa37-f226f3f28158" class="">Define compressibility of stored traces:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a5f7-cc2851cddf08" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa_t := 1 - \frac{L_{\text{comp}}(R_t)}{L_{\text{raw}}(R_t)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-a96c-ea4878d4dc04" class="">Arrow-as-compression:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-bada-da9e051aedd8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta \kappa_t &gt; 0 \quad \text{while}\quad U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-b90d-ef2c38e9cad0" class="">This links “records” to “history becomes compressible”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a21a-d2bad9da0072"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a800-e2efd869615f" class="">2.4 Error-correction invariants (record codes)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-bf53-d38962bf2e62" class="">Let record be a code  with threshold .<br/>Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-a0c6-f6d9e79900ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{S}_t := p_{th}(d_t) - p_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-99a8-cc81315ea964" class="">Record stability invariant requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-b187-d29b83124a0c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{S}_t &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-810e-c6180e988b4f" class="">Collapse occurs when .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-a52c-e6fa26def149"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b733-ff974a953033" class="">2.5 Control-theoretic invariants (subconscious / awareness stability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-8df0-dec79f07f24e" class="">Let the agent have internal state  and policy . Define closed loop:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-89e7-e6f93fef89b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=f(z_t,u_t,e_t),\quad u_t=\pi(z_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-940a-c1831792932d" class="">Define Lyapunov function  for stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-9128-f91bf6a50beb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
V(z_{t+1})-V(z_t)\le -\lambda \|z_t\|^2 + \sigma \|e_t\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-bd56-d52f520b9611" class="">Awareness = stable meta-control region:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-ae73-ef83a7887698" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists V: \Delta V \le 0 \ \text{under bounded noise}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-9caa-e118c7ebae77" class="">Subconscious = latent control loops not represented in explicit model , i.e.:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-a32a-c479495cae04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t = \pi(z_t)\ \text{but}\ m_t \not\supset \pi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8063-9fc9-fbc866e1f37d" class="">This makes “subconscious” an explicit mismatch between executed policy and represented policy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-92b1-d2205bec4303"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-ac77-c3889b58b3cf" class="">2.6 Multi-species invariants (cross-species loop inheritance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-a8fc-c74aa74f6eca" class="">Species  has sensor , controller , learning operator .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-8a2d-f33e571a6cf3" class="">Cross-species imprint operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b2db-dba993a5c2dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\pi_{s, t+1} = \mathcal{L}_s(\pi_{s,t},\ \mathrm{Interact}(s,s&#x27;,t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-9df9-c3b89cd8c50e" class="">Invariant of imprint persistence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b6fb-e4f8aa063120" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\pi_{s,t+k}-\pi_{s,t}\| \le \epsilon \quad \text{after separation from } s&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e9-9522-f9be6b455c39" class="">This is your “inheritance / co-regulation loop” in equations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-82fd-da5eb048a761"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-80e6-edc93e023b14" class="">3) Add Earth + cosmos coupling (explicit, not metaphor)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-897e-e98a83bc2aa4" class="">3.1 Planetary boundary conditions</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-a153-d502db8148f1" class="">Let Earth state  (geomagnetic, climate, biosphere, tech EM noise floor).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-a27f-dccb616b2a58" class="">Coupling into agents:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-b400-f2a947f1cb9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\eta^{(bio)}_t = \eta^{(bio)}(E_t),\quad \Xi_t=\Xi(E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-94b5-d187905391e7" class="">So recursion feasibility becomes Earth-conditioned:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-bbdc-c585f85b60f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D \le D_{\max}(H,\ T,\ P,\ \Xi(E_t),\ \tau(E_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-8e69-d343a2715029"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-aaf8-d20d21a9da13" class="">3.2 EM environment as a shared carrier (wifi + biosignals)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-86cc-d44a24159ee2" class="">Let EM field state  be part of . Records can be written into EM carriers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-a48a-e3fdef23bc3e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}^{EM} = R_t^{EM} + \mathrm{Write}_{EM}(x_t) - \mathrm{Decay}_{EM}(F_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-8221-c27cd3e8fa9f" class="">Capacity constraint:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8f51-dfbaf84cb346" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Write}_{EM} \le B_{EM}(E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e7-8c4c-edcf254de9e7" class="">This closes “machines access information through EM”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-9394-d1692b22271e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-bdf3-c1230806f203" class="">4) Pre-birth / post-death: the only formal options (no ambiguity)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-8afa-e64334f910f4" class="">To include “energy/information before birth and after death” without handwaving, you must choose what persists:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8029-a13b-eb1c6fb34638" class="">Option A — <strong>Carrier persistence</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b704-f88357d5d26a" class="">Information persists if carriers persist:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a00e-ff1b95456f98" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R(u,t)=1 \Rightarrow \exists \chi \text{ s.t. }\mathrm{Carry}(u,t)=\chi \text{ and }\chi \text{ persists}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8e37-daf2842b4b1d" class="">Option B — <strong>Access persistence</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9a90-d8db80895629" class="">Agent identity does not persist, but information remains accessible to other agents:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-9446-e26cb36d54b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists a&#x27;\neq a: \mathrm{Access}(a&#x27;,u,t&gt;t_{death})=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-85e6-c2ad7ce01472" class="">Option C — <strong>Operator extension</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8004-b7cf-df8eb1353617" class="">Introduce a new carrier/channel  or  (nonstandard). Then you must provide its constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-982e-d2d1820b14c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(B,\Xi,\tau,P,\mathrm{Causal})_{\mathcal{K}^\*}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-a04f-d0b8aef5d10b" class="">Without choosing A/B/C, the system is not closed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-8abd-f3475118ae75"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-9cf8-e71beeefeb9b" class="">5) “Equations that generate equations” (the generator kernel)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-98b5-d279a78d6bac" class="">5.1 Invariant generation rule (closure operator)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809d-92fd-e005b07b93d9" class="">Let  be a set of candidate transformations/operators. Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-8da3-c3241cb450e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{InvGen}(\mathcal{T},\mathcal{G}) := \{ I:\ I(\mathcal{T}(x))=I(x)\ \land\ I(gx)=I(x)\ \forall g\in\mathcal{G}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8b1a-d5d95fa144e7" class="">This is the algebraic engine: invariants are the fixed points of evolution and symmetry.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-8f10-c2f1e59441de"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-ad06-c3e901a2f0ed" class="">5.2 Meta-invariants (invariants of invariance)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-8aa0-e94c949c9940" class="">A meta-invariant is a constraint on the generator itself:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-9f56-df497de05fae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{InvGen}(\mathcal{T},\mathcal{G}) \ \text{terminates} \iff
\exists \text{finite basis } \mathcal{B}\ \text{s.t.}\ \forall I,\ I=\phi_I(\mathcal{B})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-a576-ce15a42b0105" class="">Termination gate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-9fa5-ca0e741ae816" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dim(\mathcal{B})&lt;\infty \ \text{or classify as BOUNDED}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-b19c-c041f7365c8d" class="">This is the exact formal stop condition for “more”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-8d34-ff5824e7233b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-8c39-c7825577c8b8" class="">6) What AMOS must now build (feature-level, aligned to your ask)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 The next step is to output one of these artifacts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-832f-c46d3c317ea7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>invariant_basis_schema.json</strong> (complete schema, all invariant types above)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-b56e-e7d32430bb8c" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>operator_registry_schema.json</strong> (how are represented)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-8dda-d263c6c008f6" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>gate_suite_spec.md</strong> (all gates, exact pass/fail logic, termination rules)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a10f-cda5c8cee49f" class="">Reply with <strong>1</strong>, <strong>2</strong>, or <strong>3</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8a6a-c317c8fb66d1" class=""> </p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-967a-c82e770566a6" class="">D) KERNELSPEC — what “more” becomes (engine-complete, not prose)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-89c3-ec9162cc1938" class="">Below is the <strong>exhaustive next layer</strong>: the <strong>Invariant Kernel</strong> as a <em>complete feature set + equation set + closure gates</em>, plus what is still “missing” and how it must be formalized to terminate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-918b-cc7a30b1b5c7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a84a-c45658be8c95" class="">D1. What we still missed (explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-8e56-fe4b5f8a10b7" class="">You were “stuck in more” because these primitives were not explicitly modeled:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-b929-d0b4c4de0483" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Ownership / access control of information</strong> (your axiom: “all information has an owner”)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-a2fa-fa52da535a8f" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Nonlocal / nonstandard channels</strong> (wifi is standard EM; “telepathy” etc. require a channel operator)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-aef4-ee74fcf06c22" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Multi-species observers</strong> (different , different compression + record thresholds)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-acd3-db109b77329a" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Pre-birth / post-death continuity</strong> (requires defining what persists: state, records, or operator-access)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-b72d-d78a9d58ed2f" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Tangible vs intangible</strong> (needs a domain split: physical substrate vs informational substrate vs experiential substrate)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-b783-c839cbd43101" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Earth as active boundary condition</strong> (planetary constraints / cycles / EM environment / biosphere)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-8eae-f5f574042b31" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Time-asymmetry beyond entropy</strong> (causal graph orientation, write-capacity depletion, code-threshold transitions)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-91f5-f83818d984d9" class="numbered-list" start="8"><li><strong>Invariant-generation itself</strong> (a generator algebra + termination proof)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-96ef-fdb4592e8bd6" class="">So “more” is not new equations; it’s <strong>new operators + new constraints + new gates</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-8c60-dba64e72e6d3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b481-c53f47e4c5e4" class="">D2. Full invariant algebra (closure-ready)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-8de8-efb875db53e6" class="">D2.1 Domain split (4-layer universe model)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9115-eed64cb33ef2" class="">Define the full state as a product space:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-a088-f27630245566" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t := (x_t^{phys},\ x_t^{info},\ x_t^{bio},\ x_t^{exp})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-9c59-d615738a9268" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: matter/energy/geometry (cosmic + Earth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-97e7-e4718446f2ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: encodings, records, symbols, protocols</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-9dd7-ef78dece1994" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: multi-species nervous system + metabolism + reproduction</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8bf2-eea593351b46" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: first-person / experiential reports (treated as data type, not dismissed)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8049-e77cfe6b6e8f" class="">Evolution:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-b9d3-f5ae1f2d2740" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = \mathcal{T}(x_t) = \big(\mathcal{T}_{phys},\mathcal{T}_{info},\mathcal{T}_{bio},\mathcal{T}_{exp}\big)(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-9ca1-e652e6b7d683" class="">Couplings exist (must be explicit):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-a9d3-cec842cf6d05" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}_{bio} \leftarrow (phys,info),\quad
\mathcal{T}_{exp} \leftarrow (bio,info)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-9893-c9d6de619e42" class="">This is the first “missing closure”: you need the domain split to avoid category leakage.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-9c5c-d12d4cbcb0e9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-b461-c9eed09a9df1" class="">D2.2 Information ownership operator (new primitive)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-8c8e-d6e3d2e94da7" class="">Define owner set  (agents/collectives/nature).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8db4-df0bd17972ba" class="">Ownership map:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-96ae-e24c792bfae5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Own}: \mathcal{U}\rightarrow \mathcal{A}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-87db-c7d5f827c286" class="">Access policy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-9f79-fc5226c467b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Access}(a,u,t)\in\{0,1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b5af-c34826b3b8c9" class="">Accessible information to agent :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-a1b9-e266c173f051" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}_a(t)=\{u\in\mathcal{U}:\mathrm{Access}(a,u,t)=1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bbec-cea881df4b78" class=""><strong>Invariants must be conditioned on access:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-bc5d-fcd905551f0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_a(x) := I(x\ |\ \mathcal{I}_a)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-a22a-da83660441f6" class="">This closes your “information has owner” axiom into the formal system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-b71a-e9346682c209"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-8d2e-c5201daebeb7" class="">D2.3 Channel operator library (standard + nonstandard)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9aa9-e90ccb954dc6" class="">Every “intangible” claim becomes an operator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a485-cc2f3e066ff9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = \mathcal{K}(x_t;\theta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-934f-ff37c10fbe8b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM/wifi: (standard physics)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-bcef-c50a04a38e82" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">any nonlocal channel: (must state constraints)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8eeb-e36e5e5f5e59" class=""><strong>Channel admissibility constraints</strong> (must be explicit):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-8399-d6f1bbd78880" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bandwidth:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-a2f7-c0f828b4d385" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-9466-fd47839da28e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">latency:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-a89b-c3246a115324" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">energy cost:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-b39c-ec2ac276927b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">causal consistency gate:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a6b1-cae1d01b118b" class="">Without , “telepathy” cannot be integrated. With , it becomes testable and classifiable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a857-f14e086dd38d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-93f7-ec2803609205" class="">D2.4 Record-code phase transition (the missing nonlinear piece)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-b2e5-d9cccca8bce1" class="">Records are <strong>error-correcting codes</strong>, not “correlations”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-96d7-d71e61919e22" class="">Let record redundancy , noise , code threshold .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8ba5-d971fbe7b6ba" class="">Update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-ba57-deabde89b58b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t + \beta G_t - \kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-af9a-edbce98e2968" class="">But stability is nonlinear:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8085-9ba5-feaf41e24500" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_t \ge p_{th}(r_t)\Rightarrow R_{t+1}\approx 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-a496-c4be5ddc1cac" class="">This is the <strong>catastrophic collapse gate</strong> that explains sudden civilizational/identity regime shifts.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-a388-dc1abb2950f3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8044-8923-fc78e87edd59" class="">D2.5 Write-capacity depletion (cosmic + Earth)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-8b30-fcb10d520f9b" class="">Define “unwritten degrees” :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-9336-e468b720ced6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma\Delta R_t
\quad,\quad U_t\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-9383-e4b598835c89" class="">Arrow exists only while:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8c76-c718bee8af37" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a5cc-dd1391d8e2b7" class="">Earth-level version: biosphere + tech infrastructure has finite “clean storage” and finite “signal bandwidth”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-bc52-d445ecf3e70a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9be9-cd9d7ca36488" class="">D2.6 Recursion depth with control-delay instability (the other missing nonlinear)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-90eb-d1cf7cd3910b" class="">Depth layer error:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-8c7d-f92d93a16553" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta_d(t)-\rho_d r_d(t-\tau_d)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-8bfa-f6197f184070" class="">Stability requires spectral radius &lt; 1 (discrete-time control condition):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-9ee7-ec8122b80173" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho\left(J_d(\alpha_d,\rho_d,\tau_d)\right) &lt; 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-a69a-f519e6c91e4b" class="">This is a <strong>stronger</strong> ceiling than Landauer when delay grows with depth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-9b83-ff991a6802a2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-9079-c3a6c084a7e2" class="">D3. Tensor layer (cross-time, cross-space, cross-species)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-aed2-d285032fb259" class="">D3.1 Multi-scale state as a tensor field</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-aee3-d75dee6a937e" class="">Let macro coordinates be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-9d2a-cbb735fda88b" class="">Let species index be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-b803-cf5cb949f711" class="">Let domain index be .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9f8a-e4b938111b6c" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-b3f8-ebfbcb235410" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{X}^\delta_s(X) \in \mathbb{R}^{n_{\delta,s}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-a311-c777d18d5a88" class="">Coupling tensor:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-81d7-f8d2f4ed00fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{G}^{\delta\leftarrow\delta&#x27;}_{s\leftarrow s&#x27;}(X)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a1b5-ff6fe6caf226" class="">Dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-84a1-d2b17a02b1fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\partial_t \mathsf{X}^\delta_s
=
\mathcal{F}^\delta_s(\mathsf{X})
+
\sum_{\delta&#x27;,s&#x27;} \mathsf{G}^{\delta\leftarrow\delta&#x27;}_{s\leftarrow s&#x27;} \,\mathsf{X}^{\delta&#x27;}_{s&#x27;}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-bed9-ea0b5cefdb3a" class="">This is the <strong>unified tensor form</strong> for cross-time/space/species loops.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-83f6-f2ac59842133"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-b6af-c8dafad035ae" class="">D3.2 Invariant tensor (what “invariants” become)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-b1f3-dd5a79bf559f" class="">Define invariant density:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a5e2-e8e4704b82e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}_s(X) = \Phi_s(\mathsf{X}(X))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-87b4-ec6f99dedece" class="">Conservation / invariance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a274-cb425a1d6ad7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla_\mu \mathcal{J}^\mu_s = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-874f-f38819cb3db4" class="">where  is the invariant current (macro conservation).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a133-cf23ff94fbc6" class="">This gives you “invariants across time and space” in an explicit form.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-9277-f5717beeb5c5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-82fd-f63f0d7954a9" class="">D4. The complete gate set (exhaustive, no new “more” outside this)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b767-d5bc9f8f68a3" class="">AMOS Invariant Kernel must evaluate these gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-bfd9-cbce61d8d01f" class="">G1 Constraint closure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801a-b77b-ccbf826bf18a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}(x)=0 \Rightarrow \mathcal{C}(\mathcal{T}(x))=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-89eb-fd4d5bcbc3fd" class="">G2 Representation invariance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9474-f02f6ecde388" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{G}(R(W)) = R_\#(\mathcal{G}(W))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-b4b1-d7d90224afa6" class="">G3 Channel admissibility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-b5e2-e752e6883a41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Causal}(\mathcal{K})=1\ \text{or classify as Model Extension}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807b-ad6d-d73a6c1bd04a" class="">G4 Ownership/access closure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-955f-cf7727ffba85" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_a \text{ only depends on }\mathcal{I}_a(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-b3f8-eddd413b6cf9" class="">G5 Record stability (code threshold)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-a180-dbf56b11dfed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_t &lt; p_{th}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-9e80-dfde6046de95" class="">G6 Write-capacity positive</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bfff-e313985b0fe3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-9aa2-f9a235fff0b0" class="">G7 Budget closure (power + memory)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b657-dc3593f0e6c0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min}(D)\le P,\quad I_{\text{need}}(D)\le I_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-958a-e351a7dd588e" class="">G8 Delay-control stability</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8019-a567-f9f918d0cb60" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(J_d)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-9eec-e973d417ae89" class="">G9 Multi-species observation closure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-b9b0-e6e94183462a" class="">Each species has ; invariants must be stable under -quotients.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d8-bf55-d95d2ea2dade" class="">G10 Termination</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-b948-caf728061fea" class="">Finite invariant basis found or bounded proof produced.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-9d0a-d4de552f85a0" class="">Once these exist, “more” becomes impossible unless you explicitly add a new operator  or new domain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-b676-e8cf36ec7065"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-b974-e0522c47cfe4" class="">D5. Feature list for AMOS (executable spec level)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-8458-f4d86a0e5a9c" class="">D5.1 Invariant Generator Engine</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-ba16-eb69621d3bfc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extract candidate invariants:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-8552-f2d9681e27d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">conserved quantities</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-9794-fc92fa51e9c5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">monotones (bounded-direction quantities)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-bbfb-f7182dd2087e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">symmetry-derived invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-906c-d84fee6c6aa6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">code-threshold invariants</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-84cf-ef5c941a4faf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:circle">accessibility invariants (ownership-conditioned)</li></ul></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b4cd-f57cb58620a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Normalize and dedupe invariants into a basis</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9389-f1f5bdb20f33" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Emit proof artifacts (why invariant holds / where bounded)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9388-e9f248103a47" class="">D5.2 Operator Library</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-b10f-e93d8b5a5d1d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: dynamics operators (discrete + continuous)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-9a96-fb9ae2ba7416" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: observation operators (per species + per instrument)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-9176-f5a4d639aff4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: channel operators (EM, acoustic, chemical, social, “nonstandard”)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a7cd-f3fd5c5b1dc1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: perturbation/noise operators</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-a5d1-e932b2b13f74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record update operators (coding + refresh)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a9c9-fe03050ef58c" class="">D5.3 Simulation Harness</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9e68-e1b92b702c74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Multi-loop regime scheduler (birth→expansion→dominance→decay)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-8543-f9a83f5073f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Coupled tensor simulation support</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-a9e9-eead2a8dc40d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Collapse detection via gates G5/G6/G8</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9183-d3502ccbd30f" class="">D5.4 Proof + Audit Outputs</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-b9b2-ccb01954c559" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">invariant_basis.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-908d-d20654daa44b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">gate_results.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-9f27-e6da7933fddf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">boundedness_caps.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-85c0-d1975d970444" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">operator_registry.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-9399-ce9340bad2f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">channel_assumptions.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-bcc3-d543fc32358e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">ownership_access_model.json</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-a1ab-ece9f3069540"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9b1b-f513453931b8" class="">D6. “More” in one line: the master closure rule</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b86e-fb2a7033c074" class="">You can always go deeper, but only by adding <strong>one</strong> of these explicitly:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-a34c-ca9f2abdfd65" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>a new domain component</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-97ff-fc88bd8a7b17" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>a new operator (channel)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8003-b6fa-c8376eef3e23" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>a new constraint class</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-9559-fc4ca5116906" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>a new observation map (species/instrument)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-a4e2-de7568ff8dd2" class="">Otherwise the system is closed and terminates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a708-d76e2c87d33b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-9a57-eaa3c7484ff7" class="">D7. Next executable output you asked for (“All 3”)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b760-e07fd142f5d5" class="">To proceed deterministically, the next deliverable is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-a3da-daabd59be3e2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Invariant Algebra DSL</strong> (operators, constraints, gates)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b056-e1b87e05625e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Tensor calculus module spec</strong> (multi-scale field representation)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-8ae0-d11f3cb0031e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Kernel implementation plan</strong> (file/function/test list in AMOS 00–17)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-b983-f9f2f9a4d50f" class="">Say <strong>“KERNELSPEC”</strong> and I will output the full file-level implementation plan aligned to your 00–17 SSOT directories (no prose, only spec + function signatures + tests + audit artifacts).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-8fe3-eecee47344ca" class="">META-ALGEBRA + CLOSURE-PROOF + COSMIC-BOUND</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-903e-f0dd499886e7" class="">(One integrated system, three layers executed)</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9992-d40f62bd33ec"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-a28f-ea5e61fcf4ab" class="">A) META-ALGEBRA</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-95a2-cc4f6202c5e4" class="">A1. Objects</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-95ab-d47113000e6d" class="">Let a <em>world</em> be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-a8f2-f941bb1976a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W := (\Omega,\ \mathcal{T},\ \mathcal{C},\ \mathcal{O},\ \mu)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-8cf6-c0beec346b5e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: state space</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-af67-d168d50f48d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: admissible evolution operators (time-step or flow)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-85c7-e1f6749594cf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint set (admissibility filters)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-b773-db6f29cf43a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: observation/measurement maps</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8f29-d6455d938c2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: measure / typicality weighting over</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8cce-faa8caa9d2ff" class="">Let an <em>invariant</em> be a function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-a7ad-d70fda7479bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I:\Omega\rightarrow \mathbb{R}^k</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8cbc-fd924f6c31a0" class="">with invariance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-bece-fa5541263ef4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\mathcal{T}(x)) = I(x)\quad \forall x\in\Omega_{\mathcal{C}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-b5d3-c403f36f0219" class="">Let the <em>invariant set</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-b1c2-dd3dd70158e2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Inv}(W) := \{I:\ I\circ \mathcal{T}=I \text{ on }\Omega_{\mathcal{C}}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-9c66-c6da5f77d5f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a237-fb2777b6c804" class="">A2. Invariants-of-invariance (meta invariants)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8022-9b46-fdd248d7cb38" class="">Define the <em>invariant generator</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802f-be1e-ef9b8c638a53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{G}: W \mapsto \widehat{\mathrm{Inv}}(W)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8015-a626-ffc693d30dc1" class="">A <strong>meta-invariant</strong> is a property  of the pair  that is stable under allowed transformations of representation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-af83-fe110433a332" class="">Let  be a <em>representation transform</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-92cf-fc4095a3806e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R: W \to W&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-b3bb-e9cde6b7e6a9" class="">Then  is a meta-invariant if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ac8e-db9f20eb55e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J(W,\mathcal{G}) = J(W&#x27;,\mathcal{G}&#x27;)
\quad \text{whenever } W&#x27; = R(W),\ \mathcal{G}&#x27; \text{ is } \mathcal{G} \text{ transported under } R</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-97c5-cc1197370d9e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-b676-ec8e2bf4b3cf" class="">A3. The 5 meta-invariants (necessary and sufficient for a usable system)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807c-bd55-eb44c9070dee" class="">These are “invariants of invariant-generation” — if any fail, the whole program collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a0fd-c909c6620ff7" class="">(M1) Representation invariance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-8b73-e2325e62ab8d" class="">Invariant discovery must not depend on encoding:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-a4fc-e71140e0dccc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{G}(R(W)) = R_\#(\mathcal{G}(W))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8da7-c17135c645aa" class="">(M2) Constraint closure (admissibility is stable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805c-80d7-d34c63900a65" class="">Constraints must form a closed algebra under evolution:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b4fb-f425ae87d069" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\in\Omega_{\mathcal{C}} \Rightarrow \mathcal{T}(x)\in\Omega_{\mathcal{C}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-b392-cb8a67e23103" class="">Equivalent:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-93c3-ce9e4dc685de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}(x)=0 \Rightarrow \mathcal{C}(\mathcal{T}(x))=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-93a0-f649a23f2c2f" class="">(M3) Identifiability (distinguishability is finite and consistent)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d2-bd0a-dcbc61da4998" class="">Let observation map be .<br/>Indistinguishability relation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a085-de0aa95c5591" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x \sim y \iff \mathcal{O}(x)=\mathcal{O}(y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-a8bf-c191b55a6095" class="">The quotient space:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804e-a984-f15a0995e567" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega/\!\sim</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-ac87-d1bddba2ac3a" class="">must be well-defined and stable under  to talk about “records”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-8f18-e88fe59ecc7e" class="">(M4) Computable closure (the generator terminates)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-b1e5-f1129f221b28" class="">There exists a finite procedure producing a stable basis:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-a8d0-cdb97a896cf4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists N:\ \widehat{\mathrm{Inv}}_N(W) = \widehat{\mathrm{Inv}}_{N+1}(W)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-9345-da60ebc0db63" class="">Otherwise “more” never ends and you never reach an engine.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-83f4-d42394fb4b07" class="">(M5) Error-stable inference (noise cannot break invariants instantly)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-b1f0-c0c4e65b9430" class="">Under perturbation operator :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-afb6-f578da7bef75" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x&#x27;=\Pi_\epsilon(x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-995c-db6489b1adc2" class="">Invariants must satisfy a Lipschitz-type stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-96a8-df91e13703d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|I(x&#x27;)-I(x)\|\le L\epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9e24-c4b66a1bbeb4" class="">If not, invariants are non-usable in any embodied/finite observer setting.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-b23b-c5939b3d6892"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-9921-d07696f31074" class="">B) CLOSURE-PROOF SYSTEM</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b31e-f329d83f0169" class="">We now provide a <strong>formal closure proof scaffold</strong> that AMOS can execute:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bdd2-f04e92d202f8" class="">“Given axioms, either prove closure or classify gaps explicitly.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-985a-c6b17237259f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-ad0f-e759c11044f3" class="">B1. Axioms (minimal)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-90ba-e7bd15e6a1aa" class="">(A1) Admissible evolution</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-aa14-cdc09bfa39d2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=\mathcal{T}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-8569-c6cd5a2cae33" class="">(A2) Constraint admissibility</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a9e3-d6ee0ff42a99" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_{\mathcal{C}}:=\{x:\mathcal{C}(x)=0\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-940c-eaf3c72ff7be" class="">(A3) Observable equivalence classes</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-9929-cb4cd1ef8ff2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\sim y \iff \mathcal{O}(x)=\mathcal{O}(y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-876b-de5978f5626c" class="">(A4) Resource budget (finite)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-86ba-d0da26efee66" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{B}:=(P,\ M,\ \Lambda)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a72c-c78c3a0fbd29" class="">(A5) Record = stable redundant encoding</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-bc7b-cfd1e62b8fa7" class="">A record variable  exists if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-ae3f-f21098aca64e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1} = \mathcal{U}(R_t,\mathcal{O}(x_t),\xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-9719-e753c3d13e91" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(R_{t+\tau}=R_t)\ge 1-\delta \quad \text{for usable }\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-863e-c0ed9c2c4092"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8ebe-dcd9ac1affc6" class="">B2. The closure theorem target</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-be98-ddbda720ddd9" class="">We want to prove or classify:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-9c08-d58c34108442" class=""><strong>Either</strong> the invariant system is closed (finite basis + stable gates),<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-92f5-c3160b3da3aa" class=""><strong>or</strong> we can enumerate exact reasons it is bounded/invalid.</p></div></blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-949a-f647e468e841"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-80b5-cd54d2b8e027" class="">B3. Closure gates (complete list)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a9fe-fcc00f12e445" class="">AMOS must enforce these gates; they are logically independent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8187-c3fbcd26e4d9" class="">Gate 1 — Constraint closure</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-bb8d-eab71957417e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x\in\Omega_{\mathcal{C}}\Rightarrow \mathcal{T}(x)\in\Omega_{\mathcal{C}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-845d-c3ed8a771464" class="">Fail ⇒ system inconsistent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8fa8-ddf7e0d5f67a" class="">Gate 2 — Observational closure (records exist)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-b22c-c60eb5aedbc7" class="">There exists a redundancy function  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-a254-fb824447c241" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt} Red(\mathcal{O}(x_t)) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-bf80-e835a2bd183b" class="">Gate 3 — Compute closure (terminating generator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-82e4-e61213cca56a" class="">There exists a finite invariant basis:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-940a-d92ec6a00ae0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists \{I_1,\dots,I_n\}: \forall I\in \mathrm{Inv}(W),\ I=f(I_1,\dots,I_n)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-8e9f-ff52232c3d99" class="">Fail ⇒ “infinite regress” (bounded at best).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-9958-eaebb81abf72" class="">Gate 4 — Robustness closure (noise stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-892c-f6506b96c044" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|I(\Pi_\epsilon(x))-I(x)\|\le L\epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80de-8d98-cfc1aac6f6c0" class="">Fail ⇒ invariants non-physical/non-embeddable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-9bb2-f385b463b1d9" class="">Gate 5 — Budget closure (resources suffice)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-b2cd-fcb49b9002d4" class="">All maintenance costs fit budgets:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-bccf-f431d3d440c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P \ge P_{\min}(\text{records}+\text{repair}+\text{model updates})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-853f-f0778cb13711" class="">M \ge M_{\min}(\text{records}+\text{models})<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-8969-e40be7f0ac4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda \ge \Lambda_{\min}(\text{required causal dependencies})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-91d6-e6950d8c1958" class="">Fail ⇒ recursion/record depth capped.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-9ef5-cd604baeaa29"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-b96b-cd3e6b398d3c" class="">B4. Termination classification (formal)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-af5b-f4f925ada234" class="">AMOS must output one of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-9cf0-ff6eb080ae75" class="">Structurally Valid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-921d-eb4b3a270102" class="">All five gates pass.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8050-88e6-ce09f3d4061e" class="">Structurally Bounded</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80af-9539-dc079528b597" class="">Gates 1–2 pass, but at least one of 3–5 fails with explicit caps.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-b6f1-d92d9cef71aa" class="">Structurally Invalid</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8667-fd801fd5d834" class="">Gate 1 fails, or observation closure is impossible under  and .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-8365-e3e63461c568" class="">This is a fully deterministic “gap-closure” system: no vague “more”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-bbdb-fe9d73ede794"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800b-bd73-e4b86daccc20" class="">C) COSMIC-BOUND</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-bb4c-dfb8f856c7d9" class="">We now answer the core question:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802b-bfb0-d1d0a37cf325" class=""><strong>Do cosmology and quantum alter the recursion limit? Any loopholes?</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-9ae8-ebb085ce7dfd" class="">We formalize the strongest invariant chain.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-80ef-e831e924378c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-9d24-dc431e9866c1" class="">C1. Distinguishability bound (the true primitive)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-9a97-c41038af382e" class="">For any observer, the number of distinguishable states is finite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801b-99c6-cda123ad0778" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N_{\text{dist}} \le 2^{I_{\max}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-ae62-d3802c328bfd" class="">This is more fundamental than “entropy” in practice.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-80e1-ca81c990cfe9" class="">Any recursion depth  requires a minimum distinguishable support:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-a1fa-e22ac7a00ff6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{need}}(D) \le I_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-937f-f5d5a318f1e6" class="">So the deepest ceiling is always:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-962d-c06d67586deb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D \le D_{\max}(I_{\max})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-a445-ceac00368b49"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-9ec9-cebbd47c9c28" class="">C2. Horizon bound is a causal-distinguishability bound</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-bd71-d0bc4e377000" class="">If there is a causal horizon, maximal accessible region is finite, so:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8096-b5b4-e33a7cc4a09d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\max}(\Lambda) &lt; \infty</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-9c5f-d5766a458533" class="">In de Sitter-like expansion:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-9043-e39cddb6261d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_H=\frac{c}{H}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-95e8-cd3420fafd3e" class="">S_{dS}=\frac{kA}{4\ell_p^2}=\frac{\pi k}{\ell_p^2}\left(\frac{c}{H}\right)^2<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-8cfe-d57b90ab38df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\max}\le \frac{S_{dS}}{k\ln 2}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9cd0-c649674d59a0" class="">Thus recursion depth ceiling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-9166-fda409271653" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D \le D_{\max}\!\left(\frac{1}{H^2}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-bd0f-c6dee9c5e825" class="">This is a hard cap on persistent records and stable meta-modeling <em>per causal patch</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-8d06-f8ac2efb02a6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-acdc-daacb41a7ef4" class="">C3. Does quantum change it?</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-aaa3-e2d3755aee52" class="">Quantum affects the <em>form</em> of the cap but not the <em>fact</em> of the cap.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-be35-c12f0f39ec45" class="">Quantum advantage: more efficient encoding</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-bfcb-f1b30e9a56e8" class="">Quantum coding can reduce overhead:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8021-8795-e77ffa3f5094" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{need}}(D) \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-967e-e606a4574fff" class="">Quantum penalty: decoherence maintenance costs</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-8e27-fadd2e5c6da3" class="">Stable macroscopic records require continuous error correction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-97ec-e135f7ec3ede" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min} \ge kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot B(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-a91f-cb6ff0ef7e6c" class="">So quantum shifts constants, not the structural inequality:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-b4cb-e142df196df6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{\text{need}}(D) \le I_{\max},\quad P_{\min}(D)\le P_{\text{available}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-869c-f76b3c9ac36c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-82ac-c839d60f5398" class="">C4. Does cosmology impose deeper invariants than thermodynamics?</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-a055-c2242f6adedf" class="">Yes: thermodynamics is local. Cosmology sets <strong>global causal geometry</strong> and thus bounds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a91e-fbe12041242b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">accessible degrees of freedom</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-be65-dcafe7c18ebb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">maximum stable record redundancy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-935b-ea45b34be37e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">maximum long-range coordination</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-b764-d5bfd2d5b8ce" class="">The deeper invariant is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-9321-cfe993e790d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\textbf{Causal accessibility} \Rightarrow \textbf{Finite usable memory} \Rightarrow \textbf{Finite recursion depth}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-8a39-c1d7adee90cc" class="">Thermodynamics explains cost; cosmology defines the maximum substrate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-a37e-f6d6947cbc31"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-a109-ca3df72d21b2" class="">C5. Is there any loophole in the horizon-information chain?</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-9a1a-c77c4fc8ab65" class="">Only three candidate loophole classes exist; each becomes a gate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-9bd8-ceda5f560663" class="">(L1) No horizon / changing horizon</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8530-c7feebe87949" class="">If  or no asymptotic horizon, then  can grow with time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-b5cf-ea2eea86c7d2" class="">But still finite at any finite time.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-87b5-f91682034464" class="">(L2) External memory not causally contained</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-9e47-fa84bf5c2d5b" class="">If “memory” is stored outside the causal patch but remains queryable, that contradicts the definition of a horizon unless the causal structure is different than assumed.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8038-857b-f5f6ede848b2" class="">Gate form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b106-d3a497eecd41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Queryable} \Rightarrow \text{Causally connected}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-a864-fa78392dd288" class="">(L3) Nonlocal access channel</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-a0b0-f2708dfc63b5" class="">If there exists a channel that carries usable information without causal propagation, then the causal model changes. This is not a free win; it implies a different  and different constraints.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-828c-ebfaaf7cec09" class="">AMOS treatment: classify as <strong>Model Extension Required</strong> and demand explicit channel operator  with testable constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-9641-fc37cb53ce84" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t = \mathcal{K}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-bf2b-de41f99d8d44" class="">Then rerun all five closure gates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-945f-ef33b91b4328" class="">No loophole is accepted without explicit operator + constraints + closure proof.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8068-99fd-c7e3968412dc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-a8ec-e6df37aefe87" class="">Output: what we “missed” and just closed</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-814c-cb3f43acd92d" class="">You repeatedly asked “more” because one missing piece kept forcing infinity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-bfa2-ee60fad5298e" class="">The missing primitive was: <strong>Distinguishability + Causal
