X_{t+\Delta t} = \mathcal{F}_L\big(X_t,\ U_t,\ \Xi_t;\ \theta\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803d-a663-c8885dff9cea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: actions/control (agents, policy, interventions)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-b14a-daf717254c6f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: stochasticity/noise (epistemic + ontic split)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b65c-eb0594cd11c0" class=""><strong>Noise split:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8332-c458e4ceee05" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t = \Xi^{ep}_t + \Xi^{on}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-b380-fc369a33c6a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-b61b-e7ccf9a2a130" class="">3) The tensor spine (time, gravity, EM, matter)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-a7c0-ceab958c742d" class="">If you want “gravity + time + tangible” in one canonical form, use the tensor layer as the physical backbone:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-af61-edeb7ece87c4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Metric encodes spacetime structure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-ae35-f5809ad6cb75" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Curvature tensors encode gravity (geometry).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-904c-da8980c52647" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Stress-energy encodes matter/energy content.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8006-b3b3-d2287df4d301" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM tensor encodes electromagnetic structure.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ef-9587-cec961f624f9" class=""><strong>Einstein equation (template, not a claim that it’s final):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-844a-fa676f2a2199" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{\mu\nu} + \Lambda g_{\mu\nu} = \frac{8\pi G}{c^4}\,T_{\mu\nu}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b9ea-fc1cb4d0a979" class=""><strong>EM dynamics:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-a7d8-e7a2f3b766d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla_\mu F^{\mu\nu} = \mu_0 J^\nu,\quad \nabla_{[\alpha}F_{\beta\gamma]}=0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-97dd-f3c699b168a4" class=""><strong>“Weyl constraint” as boundary law template:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-8acf-f29adbe62ff7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{abcd}(t_0)\approx 0 \quad \Rightarrow \quad \text{low free gravitational DOF at boundary}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-b58c-e7c463776d75" class="">This gives you a formal place to attach your “constraint-count” view.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9aea-c15dad6b5837"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-a8b2-ecef16f76510" class="">4) Laws from symmetries: “equations generate equations” via invariance</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8093-b9ad-dddc987cae34" class="">Define an action functional . The law is the stationary condition:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-9240-da37f71ffa7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\delta S = 0 \quad \Rightarrow \quad \text{Euler–Lagrange equations}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b0bd-d82a652f5224" class="">If  is invariant under a continuous symmetry, conservation laws follow:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c0-aba4-c84b4a8529bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Symmetry} \Rightarrow \text{Conserved current (Noether)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-96c5-f4458ebe3124" class="">This is literally a machine for generating laws: pick invariances → get equations + conserved quantities.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a20f-f37dac6655ee"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-8633-e40c69ca299a" class="">5) Logic layer (the “meta-law” of allowable inference)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-9d7d-f4219066d128" class="">Treat logic as constraints on admissible updates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-bbd4-f6f0b8dbb7f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Consistency gate</strong> (no contradictions in enforced claims)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-9675-f8e92bc15c47" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Support typing</strong> (EMP/INF/DEF/MODEL/PRIMITIVE/LIMIT)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ea-b6a6-c86c2fd464a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Causal admissibility</strong> (no prediction from non-causal edges)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8041-87f0-c3f6b1075c3c" class="">A compact enforcement form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8e95-cbbba5b57a27" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{G}(L)=\prod_k \mathbf{1}[\Phi_k(L)=0]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8028-b59e-c90ade102b46"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-b098-e651634ab069" class="">6) Randomness, prediction, compression (reality reconstruction requires compression)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-af35-dd97084e0165" class="">A “reconstruct reality” engine is a <strong>compression engine</strong> that predicts.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8026-9dd7-f3b8b1f349f0" class="">Let  be predictive likelihood, and  complexity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-8ddc-ffa47889885f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L^\star = \arg\max_L \big(\log p(O|L) - \lambda \Omega(L)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-b686-e72efcafaae9" class="">This is the operational form of “laws are compressive.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-81e0-ce4caf64b54b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-83ea-d359e2ec7113" class="">7) Tangible + “untangible” without hand-waving: latent channel formalism</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b39d-f41d3a117a90" class="">If you want to include “untangible” phenomena without pretending physics has validated them, you must encode them as <strong>latent variables + channel operators</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-9278-e55117f5e5c2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t = \mathcal{H}(X_t) + \mathcal{K}(Z_t) + \epsilon_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ae-aa32-e975033fda2e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: latent (untangible) state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-a6fa-d8ad5e4229ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: coupling operator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-ad74-d16d2a553ade" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">To upgrade from LIMIT → INF/EMP you must estimate and show predictive lift.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a037-fa05282ae622" class=""><strong>Channel capacity requirement:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-8311-cf4a3e42a61f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(Z;O) &gt; 0 \quad \text{and}\quad \Delta \text{PredictiveScore} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b493-ecd1c7c8ec14" class="">No coupling, no lift → classify LIMIT.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-882c-f64443a18529"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-8d34-db1af2a42e6b" class="">8) The “reconstruct reality” claim becomes a bounded theorem</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80df-8ce0-fc33fd018b37" class="">You can state this correctly as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bc10-fe1745cf30bc" class="">Given sensors , hypothesis space , constraints , and compute budget ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-8196-f9c029d34564" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists L^\star \in \mathcal{H} \;\text{s.t.}\; \mathcal{G}(L^\star)=1 \ \land\  \text{Predict}(L^\star)\ \text{meets threshold}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-941a-d3e9c99caaec" class="">But you cannot claim global completeness without quantifying  and the coverage of tests.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-81c1-e1e45c31f73a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-bdcd-ecc579ab105b" class="">9) What to build in AMOS (kernel objects)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-bf76-e1796bde22e2" class="">To implement the above, AMOS needs <strong>these canonical artifacts</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-82f8-f7b7dcd62566" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Symbol table</strong> (all variables, units, domains, layer tags)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a197-ea07567ee139" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Constraint library</strong> (dimensional, symmetry, causality, ownership, determinism)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8020-a9f8-d2eeb925634e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Hypothesis grammar</strong> (how to generate candidate equations/tensors/logic rules)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805b-8f20-e7fee6e027ee" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Scoring</strong> (fit + compression + penalties)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-ad34-e5ccadf932c8" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Search</strong> (symbolic regression / program synthesis / enumerative + pruning)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b74c-da790717015a" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Test suite</strong> (multi-modal prediction, ablations, drift closure, contradiction scans)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802a-b850-fd4d18cb0468" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Termination</strong> (Valid/Bounded/Invalid with reasons)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-96b4-e72638b42da1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a135-cceb599b6905" class="">Next (single word)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a8cd-eb125251be10" class="">If you want me to continue <em>exhaustively</em> in the direction you asked (“equations that generate equations, laws, tensors, logic, time, gravity, randomness, tangible/untangible”), choose one word:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-95a7-ddda403c2878" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>GRAMMAR</strong> → I output the full hypothesis grammar (equation/tensor/logic generation rules).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-953b-f6f60404259b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>CONSTRAINTS</strong> → I output the complete constraint library with gates and required telemetry.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-a17b-e281fcdb029a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SEARCH</strong> → I output the full law-search algorithm stack (deterministic, offline, with pruning and proofs of boundedness).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-be87-df3dbe9bb078" class="">14) Add the missing “deep layers” (F61–F90): beyond body/mind, across time/space, intangible typed, with equations + gates</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80eb-95d1-d407a5d1f3bb" class="">F61 — Multi-layer reality stack (no mixing layers without an operator)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-aa61-d263c01a2271" class="">Define layers .<br/>State is a bundle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-bbd0-cef45f53572b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t=\{x_t^{(\ell)}\}_\ell</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-8546-f1c682418d14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t^{(\ell_2)} = \Phi_{\ell_1\to \ell_2}(x_t^{(\ell_1)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-9f61-da9180e961cd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9ba2-c290108d9f2b" class="">F62 — Causality vs correlation vs narrative (typed edges)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-a142-d0da6496eb4e" class="">Graph  with edge types:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8035-a7c1-f20049c9ea30" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E = E_{\text{cause}} \cup E_{\text{corr}} \cup E_{\text{narr}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-9624-c4d760fbfc3d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat x_{t+1} = f(E_{\text{cause}}, x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-aa38-f4d962abaf27"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b97b-e28334156175" class="">F63 — Time-scale separation (micro→macro “slow manifold”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-912a-f0e2517fab50" class="">Fast , slow :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-919a-ead445952d5f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=f(x_t,y_t),\quad y_{t+1}=y_t+\epsilon g(x_t,y_t),\ \epsilon\ll 1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-a288-df7e69e304ec"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-83e4-da967ac36a45" class="">F64 — Space-scale coupling (local laws + boundary fields)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a9cc-e0f742e1c1f3" class="">Local region  embedded in global boundary :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bb6d-cf9c35e42534" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{(r)}_{t+1}=f(x^{(r)}_t; b_t),\quad b_t=\mathcal{B}(B_t,r)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808b-a9a9-ce18851cd323"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b3-a33e-dd771f687305" class="">F65 — Information accessibility ≠ information existence (channel capacity gate)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-9dbc-e4e188ccc7a7" class="">Accessible info:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-ba85-ca166dae0a6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^{acc}_t \le C_t \cdot \Delta t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-a586-e57cdec4469a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t = \max_{P(x)} I(X;Y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f5-9f2e-d40fe10a5c8b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806a-b112-c0a561c8e499" class="">F66 — Intangible observation operator (formalize without asserting mechanism)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-8611-c4e8e39dab8e" class="">Define an “intangible sensor” as an operator with reliability bounds:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8c16-d676524767b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O^{int}_t=\mathcal{O}^{int}(W_t;\sigma^{int}),\quad \Pr[O^{int}_t \text{ correct}]\ge \rho</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8001-c7deb101626a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho = \frac{1}{T}\sum_t \mathbf{1}[\hat y_t=y_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809a-9a54-dcdeba5d7f67"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a394-da9f7d3c5792" class="">F67 — Owner-bound information (permission as a conserved constraint)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801d-85e6-cc5cdbbbe1b8" class="">Ownership set . Access requires permit function :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-af27-e8885b97108c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Access(i)\Rightarrow \Pi(own(i),\text{context})=\text{ALLOW}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ba-9ceb-e4a2119e8631"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bb02-dccb4d16c738" class="">F68 — “Pre-birth / post-death” as boundary families (typed as boundary conditions)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-86b6-f7398032c8bb" class="">Boundary family  over histories:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-8f18-cf1be4745c7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x(t_0)\in \Gamma_\lambda,\quad x(t_1)\in \Gamma_{\lambda&#x27;}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b5-87e8-fe8c47d4cd94" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(\text{agent};E_{t&gt;t_1})\ge \theta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-b182-c75248fe9240"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-8727-ea4a90340d11" class="">F69 — Electromagnetic coupling loop (body ↔ environment)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-8853-caf18430df42" class="">Body EM state , environment EM :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-8d42-eac9d3becb72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_{t+1}=A e_t + B E_t + \xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9561-c42965dbd8fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{EM}_t \propto \|E_t\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-9eec-ca2ca6f76ab9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9147-f360471a2469" class="">F70 — Sensory modalities completeness (visual, auditory, interoception, proprioception)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-bbc1-e48301802752" class="">Observation vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d6-b05a-e7c3c4975245" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
O_t=(O_t^{vis},O_t^{aud},O_t^{prop},O_t^{int},O_t^{olf},O_t^{gust})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-8cce-eeadfc12cc29" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\kappa_t = \sum_{i&lt;j} I(O_t^{(i)};O_t^{(j)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d0-ab46-eecc44ea7b47"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-b561-e007dd37be5b" class="">F71 — Agency loop (choice is constrained optimization)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-8529-d30751291f7a" class="">Action:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-b5eb-e2c322814c80" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t=\arg\max_{u\in \mathcal{U}} \mathbb{E}[R(x_{t+1})]-\lambda \cdot Risk(u)-\mu \cdot Cost(u)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9737-dcb6f1fc9ef5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8037-adea-ca0f923e3f1a" class="">F72 — Ethics as a constraint operator (not sentiment)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-9a00-df652c10d1e4" class="">Ethical constraint set :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-89b4-fec8dc3e3d59" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t \in \mathcal{E}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-8ad2-e015818ba4e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
v^{eth}_t = Dist(u_t, \mathcal{E}(x_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8075-b285-f404ccd485a2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807f-be4e-e5c48aaf97b2" class="">F73 — Law engine (hard termination logic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-882d-ecff2effd7ca" class="">Termination label .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-b717-e9c43da4715c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L = \mathcal{T}( \text{gates pass}, \text{support types}, \text{contradictions})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8da3-c1fecd30e2f4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ab14-d3bd67335fb2" class="">F74 — Randomness reconstruction (separate epistemic vs ontic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-8580-fdbb611621a4" class="">Observed randomness:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8688-f9a20453bcaf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = H(O_t\mid M_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-94a1-e112d6db9365" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = r_t^{(ep)} + r_t^{(on)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-9e20-d056efde2a57" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: irreducible (model-bounded)<br/><strong>Gate:</strong> cannot claim ontic randomness without showing irreducibility under model class.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8067-a4ef-c072f56b8040"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8076-9c10-d7f56da06487" class="">F75 — Awareness as “global availability + prediction gain”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8c2a-d6f89232d2c5" class="">Broadcast variable :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-ab7a-e624779fbeb0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b_t=\mathbf{1}[I(h_t;O_t)\ge \theta]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-96e9-e0a7dafee431" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t = b_t \cdot \Delta \mathcal{S}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8023-8bbd-ef01b2c0c979"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8f46-cb2afb921ef7" class="">F76 — Self vs non-self boundary (immune-style)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-b614-c72c96a54bfe" class="">Boundary function :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-a6d8-c3b51debcff7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t=\sigma(w^\top \phi(x_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800d-8a65-f2324d9f6012" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_t = \mathbf{1}[B_t&lt;\theta_B] \cdot \text{Anomaly}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-ad41-ed797c4d94f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8045-a3ae-e4583ec4ec79" class="">F77 — Cross-species synchrony as coupling with translation operators</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8073-a9bd-f43cf6e82bd9" class="">Species  has latent . Translation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8024-aa06-f5d8203bf0d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tilde h^{(s\to s&#x27;)} = \Psi_{s\to s&#x27;}(h^{(s)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-9c14-f961984a8aae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Sync_{s,s&#x27;}(t)=I(\tilde h^{(s\to s&#x27;)};h^{(s&#x27;)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-89d5-ce49bf659e9c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-b933-e361539bf618" class="">F78 — Civilization loop as multi-constraint attractor</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-a92c-c46d10df1dc7" class="">Civilization state  with constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-ad20-f9565ed9467f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_{t+1}=F(C_t)\ \text{s.t.}\ \mathcal{E},\mathcal{K},\Pi,\mathcal{G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-b196-def25c189bb1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|C_{t+1}-C_t\|\to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8090-961f-f8f1e4d65dd1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists \text{gate}:\mathcal{G}(C_t)=0 \Rightarrow \text{phase shift}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c8-aa3d-d5abc85a94b0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-890e-cb50bb8361a6" class="">F79 — Narrative as compression with incentive bias</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-b034-c5b3933f67db" class="">Narrative  compresses events :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806d-8027-e973345581d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
n_t = \arg\min_n \; L_{comp}(n;e_{0:t})+\lambda \cdot Bias(n;inc_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-961e-e06f52d821b8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8010-9b91-c90ff3e6ad63" class="">F80 — Geopolitical “sovereign engine” as constrained control (typed MODEL)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8053-b01a-e8181d9c51d2" class="">State .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-999c-c6486a9815e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1}=F(S_t,u_t,\xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-896b-f12782f69e61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t=\pi(S_t)\ \text{s.t. compliance, ethics}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-b409-fd095344d571"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804f-b035-d20a7d0fa179" class="">F81 — Market intelligence loop (data → signal → decision → audit)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8052-ba1c-f410299567d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_t = \mathcal{F}(data_{0:t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8095-b44f-d5f3e1555241" class="">a_t = \pi(s_t)<br/><br/>Audit:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-9e8b-cbb6c664c0fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{A}_t = \{PnL_t, Risk_t, Drift_t, Violations_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-b942-d02d6972b09d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a5cc-d02a03dd73e1" class="">F82 — Drift closure (your “no gaps” requirement)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a39a-d30da3f310c1" class="">Internal delta:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-9009-d2fc83f323fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta^{int}_t=\|M_{t+1}-M_t\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b9-84b5-fe0102fe4d1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta^{fb}_t=\|O_{t+1}-\hat O_{t+1}\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8055-b9cd-f5f8e1845e73" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta^{int}_t \downarrow \ \land\ \Delta^{fb}_t \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-829a-d1572dfd92a5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-a6e0-c8ba6b965aa0" class="">F83 — Contradiction minimization as energy minimization</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9843-d13c610dbffb" class="">Let contradictions set . Define energy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-b2e3-ce921c0e2650" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{contr}(M)=\sum_{(i,j)\in\mathcal{C}} w_{ij}\cdot \mathbf{1}[M \models y_i \land y_j]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a7-b3df-f00db3fbf9d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_M E_{contr}(M)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-9f7a-efa332ba4bd0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ed-9f55-cc6acede0268" class="">F84 — “Intuition” as fast inference under learned priors (typed INF)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8057-934a-fd8efe0457b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(h\mid O)\propto p(O\mid h)p(h)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-9271-d1528edf43eb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
h^\star = \arg\max_h p(h\mid O)\ \text{computed approximately}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-9a05-d55f33c83bf7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-b8b1-ed80dcf52193" class="">F85 — Memory horizons (local, planetary, cosmic)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8043-b7dc-ceccfc83fe2b" class="">Local memory bound , horizon :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-b7c2-d180cf844ade" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{total}\le \min(I_{loc}, I_{hor})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-b588-c6b40c11fa14" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{acc}(t) \le \int_0^t C(\tau)d\tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-bcbc-df67af7f2b1a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-80cf-cf3bfd265c75" class="">F86 — Earth as a “constraint amplifier” (daily periodic forcing)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-a0a7-e15e6c3441c5" class="">Forcing term:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-807c-e3b971d2cbe2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=f(x_t)+\Gamma \sin(\omega t+\phi)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-9926-d41bff94aba3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-b82c-e7bce2b58ff9" class="">F87 — “Universe loop” as constraint evolution (Weyl → gradients → records)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-94e2-e41d18f7cc4a" class="">Keep your chain but make it a state system:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-96f0-d163d82ed425" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
(q,G,U,R,D)_{t+1} = F(q,G,U,R,D;\Xi,P,M)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-b42e-f84c21fd0b36"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-8791-e1c9b1e65c59" class="">F88 — Consciousness reconstruction stress-test (no “cannot be disproved” claim)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804c-ba27-d75bebc72474" class="">Define candidate model class . A claim “gapless” means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-bcc8-f08cdd9cc2e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall test \in \mathcal{T}: \; \text{Pass}(M,test)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bc-bd92-c6fbc1d7b142" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall\ \text{possible tests}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-afcb-e6ed88cb8bac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-961a-d69ae9582db8" class="">F89 — Digital twin of cognition (architecture variable list)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-bafc-e0ae4d047034" class="">Twin state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-ace5-f7e2cc06fce5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_t=(neural, hormonal, autonomic, sensory, memory, policy, world\_model)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-a2a9-d3ec2f852a04" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_{t+1}=F(Z_t, O_t, u_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a3b4-c648d5f8e986" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Dist(\text{behavior}(Z),\text{behavior}(\text{human}))\le \epsilon</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8042-a244-e61ce2ac4770"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a8-b910-dddbac863d20" class="">F90 — Meta-layer: “constraint-count” as the true invariant (your question)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8000-bd3f-e034af1c22ae" class="">Constraint density  and its unwind:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b340-c62e1f8d9ad2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dq}{dt}\le 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-8528-e2fb617c86b4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_t = \sum_\ell q_t^{(\ell)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b2b9-c39cbdf7b8bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta q_t = -\gamma \Delta R_t \quad \text{with}\quad U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma \Delta R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803a-b9ee-d30ee14006c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8065-9a88-e902cc45e002" class="">15) What is still missing (the remaining gaps to close)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b2-96ae-c5526c2b7c7f" class="">You have not yet defined, explicitly, these operators (required to be exhaustive):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-883b-e904606cb84a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Layer translation operators</strong> for every pair you rely on.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-a860-db047600c27d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Intangible channel model</strong> that converts “accessible” into testable.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d4-9e63-d17995d1d096" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Consciousness test suite</strong> (behavioral + internal consistency + memory + prediction).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-a850-e5e79ea4188f" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Owner/provenance enforcement</strong> as a runnable gate (not just principle).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c5-8c31-e6854f4b2fd1" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Macro↔micro closure test</strong> for your chosen coarse-grainings.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-b552-e61d5a82b160"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-9d04-fe3b16b6b6bc" class="">16) Next output (choose one word; I will produce it fully)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808d-aafc-e05d5bd811c7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>CATALOG</strong> — F0–F90 consolidated with symbol table + gates + test hooks.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-aff3-c7534b17ac53" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>OPERATORS</strong> — a complete list of all operators () with signatures and required telemetry.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-9029-fcf536524e6a" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>TESTS</strong> — a full stress-test suite including visual/sound prediction, contradiction tests, drift closure, and cross-layer consistency.</li></ol></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
